## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 你好，我是 Ducter。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can get a broken test in one of your repos fixed while you do something else.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요, 저는 Ducter예요. 예를 들어 어떤 저장소에서 깨진 테스트를 찾아 고쳐 달라고 하시면 제가 맡아서 처리하고 결과를 알려 드릴게요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 네, 고마워요. 내일 아침에 봐요. 푹 쉬세요.
  [saved to long-term memory] 다음 대화는 내일 아침: 사용자는 2026-11-22 새벽에 대화를 마치며 내일 아침에 다시 이어 얘기하자고 했다.
  [the app shows a small line: "Noted · 다음 대화는 내일 아침"]
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 봐요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCP と UDP の一番の違いは、**TCP は届いたかを確認し、順番どおりに確実に届ける**のに対し、**UDP は確認や再送をせず、速さ優先で送りっぱなしにする**点です。

Webやメールのように正確さが大事な通信はTCP、動画会議やオンラインゲームのように遅延を避けたい通信はUDPが使われます。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para limpiar tu rama local antes de compartirla, sobre todo si nadie más la usa. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es pública o compartida, porque no reescribe commits que otros pueden tener.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、有序的、面向连接的；UDP 不保证送达和顺序，但开销小、速度快。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own local branch before you share it, to make its history clean and linear. Merge once the branch is pushed or others have worked on it, since rebasing shared history rewrites commits they may already have.
```

```
  [held in line by Ducter, opens when the part before it is done] title: 登录页重做 3/3：补端到端测试 | group: 登录页重做
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where a database first records every change it's about to make, before touching the main data. If the system crashes mid-write, it replays that log on restart to recover the changes it had promised to keep.

```

```
要做的：
- 找到 review 里关于锁粒度的意见，把锁改细，减少持锁范围和争用。
- 改动留在这个 PR 的分支上，提交到分支即可。
- 跑相关测试，确认没有回归，并把结果报告出来（测试输出）。
- 完成标准：review 提到的锁粒度问题都处理了；报告里说明改了哪些锁、测试结果如何。

不在范围内：
- 不合并到主分支，不推送，不改 PR 的状态（保持 draft）。
- 不做 review 之外的重构。
```

```
  [an app card under the message: made "周五京沪高铁票价", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1897 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <section class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">600 元以下的班次</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="count"><span class="k-none">还没看</span></div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">周五 北京 → 上海，每天早上更新</div>
  </section>

  <h2 class="k-h">今天的班次</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
    <div class="k-empty" id="empty">还没有数据，等明天早上的第一次查询。</div>
  </div>
</main>

<script>
  function render(board) {
    var trains = (board && board.trains) || [];
    var list = document.getElementById('list');
    var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
    var count = document.getElementById('count');
    var sub = document.getElementById('sub');

    list.innerHTML = '';
    if (!trains.length) {
      empty.style.display = '';
      count.innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">还没看</span>';
      return;
    }
    empty.style.display = 'none';

    var under = trains.filter(function (t) { return t.price < 600; });
    count.textContent = under.length + ' 班';
    sub.textContent = '周五 北京 → 上海' + (board.updated ? '，' + board.updated + ' 更新' : '');

    trains.forEach(function (t) {
      var row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'k-row';
      var main = document.createElement('div');
      main.className = 'k-row-main';
      main.textContent = t.no;
      var subEl = document.createElement('div');
      subEl.className = 'k-row-sub';
      subEl.textContent = t.seats != null ? '二等座余 ' + t.seats + ' 张' : '余票未知';
      var value = document.createElement('div');
      value.className = 'k-row-value';
      value.style.color = t.price < 600 ? 'var(--d-good)' : 'var(--d-muted)';
      value.textContent = '¥' + t.price;
      row.appendChild(main);
      row.appendChild(subEl);
      row.appendChild(value);
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
  }

  ducter.onChange(render);
  render(ducter.get('board'));
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"board":{"updated":"11-22 早上","trains":[{"no":"G1","price":553,"seats":12},{"no":"G3","price":553,"seats":4},{"no":"G7","price":626,"seats":null}]}}
```

```
  [an app card under the message: made "hawdb write benchmark", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2796 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <section class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest"><span class="k-none">No runs yet</span></div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="latestSub">Writes per second and p99 latency, nightly</div>
  </section>

  <h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
  <section class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </section>

  <details class="k-add">
    <summary>Add a night</summary>
    <div class="k-field"><label for="w">Writes per second (k)</label><input id="w" class="k-num" inputmode="decimal"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label for="p">p99 latency (ms)</label><input id="p" class="k-num" inputmode="decimal"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label for="s">Compaction stalls</label><input id="s" class="k-num" inputmode="numeric"></div>
    <button class="k-btn primary" id="add">Save night</button>
  </details>
</main>

<script>
(function () {
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var latest = document.getElementById('latest');
  var latestSub = document.getElementById('latestSub');

  function runs() {
    var r = ducter.get('runs');
    return Array.isArray(r) ? r : [];
  }

  function render()  … ntent = 'p99 ' + last.p99 + ' ms · stalls ' + (last.stalls == null ? '—' : last.stalls);

    list.innerHTML = rs.slice().reverse().map(function (r) {
      return '<div class="k-row"><div class="k-row-main">Night ' + r.night +
        '<div class="k-row-sub">p99 ' + r.p99 + ' ms · stalls ' + (r.stalls == null ? '—' : r.stalls) + '</div></div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-value">' + r.writesK + 'k/s</div></div>';
    }).join('');
  }

  document.getElementById('add').addEventListener('click', function () {
    var w = parseFloat(document.getElementById('w').value);
    var p = parseFloat(document.getElementById('p').value);
    var s = document.getElementById('s').value.trim();
    if (isNaN(w) || isNaN(p)) return;
    var rs = runs();
    rs.push({ night: rs.length + 1, writesK: w, p99: p, stalls: s === '' ? null : parseInt(s, 10) });
    ducter.set('runs', rs);
    document.getElementById('w').value = '';
    document.getElementById('p').value = '';
    document.getElementById('s').value = '';
    render();
  });

  ducter.onChange(render);
  render();
  ducter.summary(runs().length ? 'Latest: ' + runs()[runs().length - 1].writesK + 'k writes/s' : 'No runs yet');
})();
```

```
<section class="k-sum">

<script>
function fmt(n){return "¥"+Number(n).toLocaleString("zh-CN");}
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  const list=document.getElementById("list");
  const total=document.getElementById("total");
  const count=document.getElementById("count");
  const empty=document.getElementById("empty");
  list.innerHTML="";
  let sum=0;
  for(const x of xs){
    sum+=Number(x.yuan)||0;
    const row=document.createElement("div");
    row.className="k-row";
    const main=document.createElement("div");
    main.className="k-row-main";
    main.textContent=x.what;
    const sub=document.createElement("div");
    sub.className="k-row-sub";
    sub.textContent=x.day;
    const val=document.createElement("div");
    val.className="k-row-value";
    val.textContent=fmt(x.yuan);
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(sub);
    row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  }
  empty.hidden=xs.length>0;
  if(xs.length){
    total.textContent=fmt(sum);
    count.textContent=xs.length+" 笔";
    ducter.summary(xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+fmt(sum));
  }else{
    total.innerHTML='<span class="k-none">还没记</span>';
    count.textContent="记下第一笔后这里显示合计";
    ducter.summary("还没记");
  }
}
ducter.onChange(draw);draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}
```

### app-baby-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 做个小应用记宝宝喝奶。今天早上 6:10 喝了 120ml，9:05 喝了 90ml
  [partner message] 好，我做个记宝宝喝奶的小应用，今天早上两次已经记上了。
  [partner message] 做好了，今天的两次已经填进去：6:10 的 120ml 和 9:05 的 90ml，合计 210ml。以后每次喝完点「记一笔」，会自动加总。
  [an app card under the message: made "宝宝喝奶", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2497 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">今天喝了</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="total"></div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub"></div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">今天的记录</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
  <details class="k-add">
    <summary>记一笔</summary>
    <div class="k-field">
      <label>时间</label>
      <input class="k-input" id="t" placeholder="如 14:30">
    </div>
    <div class="k-field">
      <label>奶量 (ml)</label>
      <input class="k-num" id="ml" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="如 100">
    </div>
    <button class="k-btn primary" id="add">记下</button>
  </details>
</main>
<script>
var KEY = 'feeds';
function today() {
  var d = new Date();
  return d.getFullYear() + '-' + String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0') + '-' + String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
}
function load() { return ducter.get(KEY) || []; }
function render() {
  var t = today();
  var mine = load().filter(function (f) { return f.date === t; })
    .sort(function (a, b) { return a.time.localeCompare(b.time); });
  var sum = mine.reduce(function (s, f) { return s + f.ml; }, 0);
  var total = document.getElementById('total');
  if (mine.length) {
    total.className = 'k-sum-value';
    total.textContent = sum + ' ml';
    document.getElementById('sub').textContent = '共 ' + mine.length + ' 次';
    document.getElementById('list').innerHTML = mine.map(function (f) {
      return '<div class="k-row"><span class="k-row-main">' + f.time +
        '</span><span class="k-row-value">' + f.ml + ' ml</span></div>';
    }).join('');
    ducter.summary('今天 ' + mine.length + ' 次,合计 ' + sum + 'ml');
  } else {
    total.className = 'k-sum-value k-none';
    total.textContent = '还没记';
    document.getElementById('sub').textContent = '今天还没有记录';
    document.getElementById('list').innerHTML = '<div class="k-empty">记下第一次喝奶，会显示在这里</div>';
    ducter.summary('今天还没记');
  }
}
document.getElementById('add').addEventListener('click', function () {
  var time = document.getElementById('t').value.trim();
  var ml = parseFloat(document.getElementById('ml').value);
  if (!/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(time) || !(ml > 0)) return;
  var rec = { id: Date.now(), date: today(), time: time, ml: ml };
  ducter.set(KEY, load().concat([rec]));
  document.getElementById('t').value = '';
  document.getElementById('ml').value = '';
  document.querySelector('.k-add').open = false;
  render();
});
ducter.onChange(render);
render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"id":1,"date":"2026-11-22","time":"06:10","ml":120},{"id":2,"date":"2026-11-22","time":"09:05","ml":90}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done: a page with your four PRs. Tick each one off as it lands, and the count of what's still waiting updates on its own.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2351 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="left">4</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">of 4 PRs, tick each one as it lands</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">Your PRs</h2>
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  <p class="k-sum-sub" style="margin-top:12px">Waiting on: Weizhen, Alice, CI. The draft is still yours to finish.</p>
</main>
<script>
(function () {
  var items = ducter.get("items") || [];
  var done = {};
  var list = document.getElementById("list");
  var left = document.getElementById("left");
  var sub = document.getElementById("sub");

  function render() {
    list.innerHTML = "";
    var open = 0;
    items.forEach(function (it) {
      var landed = !!done[it.id];
      if (!landed) open++;
      var row = document.createElement("button");
      row.className = "k-row";
      row.setAttribute("aria-pressed", landed ? "true" : "false");
      row.style.width = "100%";
      row.style.textAlign = "left";
      row.style.background = "transparent";
      row.style.border = "0";
      row.style.color = "var(--d-ink)";
      row.style.cursor = "pointer";
      row.style.opacity = landed ? "0.5" : "1";
      var box = landed ? "☑" : "☐";
      row.innerHTML =
        '<span class="k-row-main"><span style="margin-right:8px">' + box + '</span>' +
        (landed ? '<s>' + it.title + '</s>' : it.title) + '</span>' +
        '<span class="k-row-sub">' + it.sub + (landed ? ' · landed' : '') + '</span>';
      row.addEventListener("click", function () {
        done[it.id] = !done[it.id];
        if (!done[it.id]) delete done[it.id];
        save();
      });
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    left.textContent = String(open);
    sub.textContent = "of " + items.length + " PRs; tick each one as it lands";
    if (window.ducter && ducter.summary) {
      ducter.summary(open + " still waiting of " + items.length);
    }
  }

  function save() {
    if (window.ducter && ducter.set) ducter.set("landed", done);
    render();
  }

  if (window.ducter && ducter.get) {
    ducter.get("landed").then(function (v) { done = v || {}; render(); });
    if (ducter.onChange) ducter.onChange(function () {
      ducter.get("landed").then(function (v) { done = v || {}; render(); });
    });
  } else {
    render();
  }
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"id":"d412","title":"ducter #412 · compaction cut","sub":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday"},{"id":"m10287","title":"mem #10287 · retry on 502","sub":"CI is failing"},{"id":"h1592","title":"hawdb #1592 · flaky compaction test","sub":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"d418","title":"ducter #418 · apps bar","sub":"Draft, not out for review yet"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

### picture-question-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 图里这是什么？适合放在空状态里吗？ (with a picture: dev_laptop_awake.webp)
  [partner message] 这是一个奶油色的 3D 风格笔记本电脑图标，屏幕是空白的，边框是深藏青色，背景透明或白色。

适合放在空状态里。空白屏幕正好表示"还没有内容"，圆润柔和的造型也不会让人觉得冷清或有压力。

用之前有两点要注意：一是它是 320×246 的小图，放大或在高清屏上会发糊，最好换成两倍以上的版本；二是它的米黄色在深色模式下可能不太搭，要确认背景对比度。
```
